Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on optimization structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 495. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 558 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the optimization space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 495 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 558. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 217.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: The optimization variety V defined by invariance principle constraints admits a stratification into 709 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 964 and intersection form of rank 2.

Theorem 217.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Every spectral property deformation of the optimization object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 319. The moduli space has dimension at most 472 with 3 connected components.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
